  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "The relations defining the probe give two factorizations of the prescribed common multiple $ae$. Each arrow means right multiplication by its label, not a covering relation. This is not a Hasse diagram: path points may coincide with $a$, and $a$ may divide $m$. The exact-profile assertion is that a divisor of $a$ omitted from the path’s downward closure has join $ae$ with $m$.",
      "figure_id": "fig:probe-divisibility",
      "pdf_page": 8,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:probe-divisibility",
          "exact_quote": "The relations defining the probe give two factorizations of\nthe prescribed common multiple $ae$.  Each arrow means right\nmultiplication by its label, not a covering relation.  This is not a\nHasse diagram: path points may coincide with $a$, ",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[font=\\small,>=Stealth]\n  \\node (pathpoint) at (0,0) {$n_i$};\n  \\node (oldpoint) at (3,1) {$a$};\n  \\node (probe) at (3,-1) {$m=p_0$};\n  \\node (commonmultiple) at (6,0) {$ae$};\n  \\draw[->] (pathpoint) -- node[above left] {$c_i$} (oldpoint);\n  \\draw[->] (pathpoint) -- node[below left] {$p_i$} (probe);\n  \\draw[->] (oldpoint) -- node[above right] {$e$} (commonmultiple);\n  \\draw[->] (probe) -- node[below right] {$b$} (commonmultiple);\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A directed commutative-style diagram with four vertices arranged in a diamond: vertex $n_i$ on the left, vertex $a$ at the top, vertex $m = p_0$ at the bottom, and vertex $ae$ on the right. Directed arrows connect $n_i \\to a$ labeled with $c_i$, $n_i \\to m = p_0$ labeled with $p_i$, $a \\to ae$ labeled with $e$, and $m = p_0 \\to ae$ labeled with $b$."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The independent-copy case of support descent. Each edge is labelled by the exact intersection of its endpoint ranges. The two placed equality witnesses $v′, v″$ use the same fresh copy $u$ of $s_{1}$; $t$ carries the proposed inequality between the copies on $s̄$ and $s̄′$, whose ranges meet exactly in $D_{p}$. These exact intersections allow the three diagrams to be amalgamated over their common root $p$.",
      "figure_id": "fig:support-descent",
      "pdf_page": 21,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:support-descent",
          "exact_quote": "The independent-copy case of support descent. Each edge is\nlabelled by the exact intersection of its endpoint ranges. The two\nplaced equality witnesses $v',v''$ use the same fresh copy $u$ of $s_1$;\n$t$ carries the proposed inequality betwe",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[font=\\small,\n    diagramrange/.style={draw,rounded corners=2pt,fill=white,\n                  inner xsep=9pt,inner ysep=5pt},\n    overlap/.style={fill=white,inner sep=3pt}]\n  \\node[diagramrange] (t) at (0,1.8) {$D_t$};\n  \\node[diagramrange] (v) at (-3,-.8) {$D_{v'}$};\n  \\node[diagramrange] (w) at (3,-.8) {$D_{v''}$};\n  \\draw (t) -- node[overlap,pos=.52] {$D_{\\bar s}$} (v);\n  \\draw (t) -- node[overlap,pos=.52] {$D_{\\bar s'}$} (w);\n  \\draw (v) -- node[overlap] {$D_u$} (w);\n  \\node[align=center,font=\\footnotesize] at (0,-.15)\n    {$D_p$\\\\triple intersection};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A triangular diagram connecting three boxed nodes: $D_t$ at the top apex, $D_{v'}$ at the bottom-left corner, and $D_{v''}$ at the bottom-right corner. The three edges forming the triangle are labeled by intersections: the edge between $D_t$ and $D_{v'}$ is labeled $D_{\\bar{s}}$; the edge between $D_t$ and $D_{v''}$ is labeled $D_{\\bar{s}'}$; and the horizontal base edge between $D_{v'}$ and $D_{v''}$ is labeled $D_u$. In the interior region of the triangle, curves converge toward a label with two lines: \"$D_p$\" and \"triple intersection\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "Transporting a preliminary bijection, for $π ∈ G_{C}$. The square commutes: to assign the bottom label $j$, first compute at $π^{−1}j$ in the generic $π^{−1}F$, then transport the result to $F$. The top and bottom computations use $π^{−1}F$ and $F$, respectively.",
      "figure_id": "fig:moved-generic-transport",

      "source_evidence": [

          "anchor": "fig:moved-generic-transport",
          "exact_quote": "Transporting a preliminary bijection, for $\\pi\\in G_C$.\nThe square commutes: to assign the bottom label $j$, first compute\nat $\\pi^{-1}j$ in the generic $\\pi^{-1}F$, then transport the result\nto $F$. The top and bottom computations use $\\pi",
          "source_object": "fig:0003"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[font=\\small,>=Stealth]\n  \\node (a) at (0,1.65) {$L_C$};\n  \\node (b) at (6,1.65) {$B_C(\\pi^{-1}F)$};\n  \\node (c) at (0,0) {$L_C$};\n  \\node (d) at (6,0) {$B_C(F)$};\n  \\draw[->] (a) -- node[above] {$f_C^0(\\pi^{-1}F)$} (b);\n  \\draw[->] (a) -- node[left] {$\\pi\\restriction L_C$} (c);\n  \\draw[->] (b) -- node[right] {$T_\\pi^{\\pi^{-1}F}$} (d);\n  \\draw[->] (c) -- node[below] {$f_C^{\\pi K_C}(F)$} (d);\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A commutative square diagram with four vertices: $L_C$ at top-left, $B_C(\\pi^{-1}F)$ at top-right, $L_C$ at bottom-left, and $B_C(F)$ at bottom-right. Four directed arrows connect the vertices: a horizontal arrow from $L_C$ to $B_C(\\pi^{-1}F)$ labeled $f_C^0(\\pi^{-1}F)$; a horizontal arrow from $L_C$ to $B_C(F)$ labeled $f_C^{\\pi K_C}(F)$; a downward vertical arrow from top-left $L_C$ to bottom-left $L_C$ labeled $\\pi \\upharpoonright L_C$; and a downward vertical arrow from top-right $B_C(\\pi^{-1}F)$ to bottom-right $B_C(F)$ labeled $T_\\pi^{\\pi^{-1}F}$."
